of he Apollo ype provides he guidance, con rol, and communica ion



func ions; i will be capable of separa ion, re-en ry and recovery as



well as abor func ions.

The in erac ion of he elas ic missile fuselage, feedback con rol sys em,

di ions .



To minimize he effec s of he propellan oscil-



la ions, several me hods may be employed. The proposed me hod is he



in roduc ion of annular baffles, rigidly a ached o he ank walls, o



dis urb he flow near he free surface and crea e enough urbulence o

If annular ring baffles are used, as he presen 



design indica es, he op imum configura ion and loca ion should be de-



 ermined. The damping due o a ring baffle has been shown analy ically



(Reference A), and experimen ally (Reference B) o decrease wi h he



dep h of he baffle below he surface. The baffle loca ion necessary



for subs an ial damping is limi ed o a narrow range below he surface



so ha he op imum spacing of he baffles is dependen on he cri ical



dep hs of he liquid; i.e., he dep h of fluid resul ing in a resonan 



frequency or a condi ion demanding an increase in damping.



Reference A : "Ring Damping of Free Surface Oscilla ions in a Circular



Tank", Miles, Journal of Applied Mechanics, Vol.25, No. 2.
